first.

</pre>The verifier is an instance of the generic trace verifier
`CategoricalCrypto.Machine.Trace` for `LinearLeios`: the hint naming a step's
rule is its `Action`, and traces name inputs as `TestInput`s, mapped into the
machine's input by `toRcvType`.  A `StepWith a s i` is a step from `s` on input
`i` whose rule is `a`.
<pre class="Agda"><a id="StepWith"></a><a id="3859" href="Leios.Linear.Trace.Verifier.html#3859" class="Function">StepWith</a> <a id="3868" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="3870" href="Leios.Linear.Trace.Verifier.html#718" class="Datatype">Action</a> <a id="3877" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="3879" href="Leios.Protocol.html#1671" class="Record">LeiosState</a> <a id="3890" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="3892" href="Leios.Linear.Trace.Verifier.html#1295" class="Function">TestInput</a> <a id="3902" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="3904" href="Agda.Primitive.html#388" class="Primitive">Type</a>
<a id="3909" href="Leios.Linear.Trace.Verifier.html#3859" class="Function">StepWith</a> <a id="3918" href="Leios.Linear.Trace.Verifier.html#3918" class="Bound">a</a> <a id="3920" href="Leios.Linear.Trace.Verifier.html#3920" class="Bound">s</a> <a id="3922" href="Leios.Linear.Trace.Verifier.html#3922" class="Bound">i</a> <a id="3924" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="3926" href="Data.Product.Base.html#1244" class="Function">Σ[</a> <a id="3929" href="Leios.Linear.Trace.Verifier.html#3929" class="Bound">x</a> <a id="3931" href="Data.Product.Base.html#1244" class="Function">∈</a> <a id="3933" href="CategoricalCrypto.Machine.Trace.html#2711" class="Record">T.Step</a> <a id="3940" href="Leios.Linear.html#15590" class="Function">LinearLeios</a> <a id="3952" href="Leios.Linear.Trace.Verifier.html#3920" class="Bound">s</a> <a id="3954" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="3955" href="Leios.Linear.Trace.Verifier.html#3291" class="Function">toRcvType</a> <a id="3965" href="Leios.Linear.Trace.Verifier.html#3922" class="Bound">i</a><a id="3966" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="3968" href="Data.Product.Base.html#1244" class="Function">]</a> <a id="3970" href="Leios.Linear.Trace.Verifier.html#1493" class="Function">getAction</a> <a id="3980" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="3981" href="CategoricalCrypto.Machine.Trace.html#2847" class="Field">T.derivation</a> <a id="3994" href="Leios.Linear.Trace.Verifier.html#3929" class="Bound">x</a><a id="3995" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="3997" href="Agda.Builtin.Equality.html#150" class="Datatype Operator">≡</a> <a id="3999" href="Leios.Linear.Trace.Verifier.html#3918" class="Bound">a</a>
</pre>### Error handling
`Err-InputMismatch` carries the real refutation `¬ StepWith σ s i`.  Deriving it requires
inverting the transition's input index `toRcvType i`, whose channel selections are `opaque` in
categorical-crypto; the lemmas below therefore sit in an `opaque unfolding _⊗₀_` block, where
the selections reduce to constructor form and Agda can dismiss the impossible transition
rules.  The refutation is mediated by the *input-channel selector*: `input-sound` proves that
every derivable step consumes the input constructor its action's rule expects, so a selector
mismatch refutes the step.

The premise-less `Fetch` rule reads its input through an output-typed channel selection, which
nevertheless coincides with `toRcvType (inj₂ (inj₂ (inj₁ FetchLdgI)))` once the selections reduce;
`Fetch-step` witnesses this inside the unfolding block, letting `verifyStep'` accept the pairing.

</pre>Each error names the action, and where it matters the input, that it
rejects, so that it refutes every step the trace entry could stand for
(see `refute-rule` below).  An EB-Role error is stated for the verifier's
VRF proof `π₀ s`, the only one `canProduceEB` admits (`π-unique`), and a
VT-Role error for the hash of the voted EB, the only hash `CanVote` admits.

</pre>`verifyStep` is the generic verifier's step checker.  Outputs are not
checked here; the generic `passes?` checks them separately, against an
`Observation`.

<a id="20196" href="Leios.Linear.Trace.Verifier.html#20168" class="Function">toTrace</a> <a id="20204" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="20206" href="Data.List.Base.html#1612" class="Function">L.map</a> <a id="20212" class="Symbol">λ</a> <a id="20214" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="20215" href="Leios.Linear.Trace.Verifier.html#20215" class="Bound">a</a> <a id="20217" href="Agda.Builtin.Sigma.html#235" class="InductiveConstructor Operator">,</a> <a id="20219" href="Leios.Linear.Trace.Verifier.html#20219" class="Bound">i</a><a id="20220" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="20222" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="20224" href="CategoricalCrypto.Machine.Trace.html#5724" class="InductiveConstructor Operator">⟨</a> <a id="20226" href="Leios.Linear.Trace.Verifier.html#20215" class="Bound">a</a> <a id="20228" href="CategoricalCrypto.Machine.Trace.html#5724" class="InductiveConstructor Operator">,</a> <a id="20230" href="Leios.Linear.Trace.Verifier.html#20219" class="Bound">i</a> <a id="20232" href="CategoricalCrypto.Machine.Trace.html#5724" class="InductiveConstructor Operator">⟩</a>
</pre>### Completeness
The verifier is also complete: every error refutes all steps with the
entry's action, and an action determines the output and target of its
step.  The only two rules sharing an action, `No-VT-Role` and `VT-Defer`,
have the same output and target.  Hence `validTrace? checkable-complete`
decides `ValidTrace`.
